00035: def wood():
00036:     t = random.random()
00037:     return (.28 + .30*t, .14 + .21*t, .055 + .085*t)
00038: 
00039: def put(parent, key, node):
00040:     parent.include(key, node)
00041:     return node
00042: 
00043: def block(parent, key, size, center, color):
00044:     return put(parent, key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00045: 
00046: def bar(parent, key, start, end, width, depth, color):
00047:     dx, dy, dz = (end[i]-start[i] for i in range(3))
00048:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
00049:     center = tuple((start[i]+end[i])/2 for i in range(3))
00050:     node = box((width, depth, length), center=center, color=color)
00051:     if abs(dy) < 1e-8:
00052:         node.turn('y', math.degrees(math.atan2(dx, dz)), pivot=center)
00053:     else:
00054:         node.turn('x', -math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dz)), pivot=center)
00055:     return put(parent, key, node)

00414: # Initialize shader colors explicitly from SceneAct's stored finish.
00415: # Enabling nodes on a diffuse-only material can otherwise leave a default
00416: # pale Principled color, which also corrupts wood/metal classification.
00417: for mat in bpy.data.materials:
00418:     finish = tuple(mat.diffuse_color[:3])
00419:     mat.use_nodes = True
00420:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00421:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00422:     bsdf = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00423:     if bsdf is None:
00424:         continue
00425:     for link in list(bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].links):
00426:         links.remove(link)
00427:     r,g,b = finish
00428:     is_wood = r > g*1.23 and g > b*1.25 and r > .16
00429:     is_metal = max(finish)-min(finish) < .095 and r < .49
00430: 
00431:     # Dark stained timber and worn iron carry most of the photograph's contrast.
00432:     if is_wood:
00433:         base = (r*.66,g*.57,b*.48)
00434:     elif is_metal:
00435:         base = tuple(v*.62 for v in finish)
00436:     else:
00437:         base = finish
00438:     bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*base,1)
00439:     bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .76
00440:     bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = .55 if is_metal else 0
00441:     if is_wood:
00442:         coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00443:         stretch = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00444:         stretch.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00445:         stretch.inputs[1].default_value = (2.5,29,5)
00446:         tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00447:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 3.8
00448:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00449:         links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],stretch.inputs[0])
00450:         links.new(stretch.outputs["Vector"],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00451:         ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00452:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .17
00453:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*[v*.65 for v in base],1)
00454:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .84
00455:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*[min(v*1.22,1) for v in base],1)
00456:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00457:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
00458:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00459:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .12
00460:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .012
00461:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00462:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bsdf.inputs["Normal"])
00463:     elif is_metal:
00464:         bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .58

00026:     box((12, 12, .10), center=(0, 0, -.07), color=(.15, .075, .042)))

00122: # Fine rounded perimeter piping.
00123: def rounded_outline(x0, x1, y0, y1, radius, z):
00124:     result = []
00125:     for cx, cy, start in [
00126:         (x1-radius, y1-radius, 0),
00127:         (x0+radius, y1-radius, 90),
00128:         (x0+radius, y0+radius, 180),
00129:         (x1-radius, y0+radius, 270)
00130:     ]:
00131:         for j in range(9):
00132:             a = math.radians(start+j*90/8)
00133:             result.append((cx+radius*math.cos(a), cy+radius*math.sin(a), z))
00134:     result.append(result[0])
00135:     return result

00305: # Procedural woven upholstery with fine faded rose lines and tiny repeated marks.
00306: def make_fabric(name, base, patterned=False):
00307:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00308:     mat.use_nodes = True
00309:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00310:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00311:     bs = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00312:     bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*base, 1)
00313:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .85
00314:     if "Sheen Weight" in bs.inputs:
00315:         bs.inputs["Sheen Weight"].default_value = .18
00316: 
00317:     geo = nodes.new("ShaderNodeNewGeometry")
00318:     noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00319:     noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 260
00320:     noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00321:     links.new(geo.outputs["Position"], noise.inputs["Vector"])
00322:     bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00323:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .17
00324:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .0017
00325:     links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00326:     links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00327: 
00328:     if patterned:
00329:         def mathnode(op, a=None, value=None):
00330:             q = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00331:             q.operation = op
00332:             if a is not None:
00333:                 links.new(a, q.inputs[0])
00334:             if value is not None:
00335:                 q.inputs[1].default_value = value
00336:             return q.outputs[0]
00337: 
00338:         dx = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00339:         dx.operation = "DOT_PRODUCT"
00340:         dx.inputs[1].default_value = (.73135, -.682, 0)
00341:         links.new(geo.outputs["Position"], dx.inputs[0])
00342:         dy = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00343:         dy.operation = "DOT_PRODUCT"
00344:         dy.inputs[1].default_value = (.682, .73135, 0)
00345:         links.new(geo.outputs["Position"], dy.inputs[0])
00346: 
00347:         yphase = mathnode("MULTIPLY", dy.outputs["Value"], 138)
00348:         ysin = mathnode("SINE", yphase)
00349:         yabs = mathnode("ABSOLUTE", ysin)
00350:         stripe = mathnode("LESS_THAN", yabs, .055)
00351:         stripe = mathnode("MULTIPLY", stripe, .22)
00352: 
00353:         rows = mathnode("MULTIPLY", dy.outputs["Value"], 69)
00354:         rows = mathnode("SINE", rows)
00355:         rows = mathnode("GREATER_THAN", rows, .94)
00356:         dashes = mathnode("MULTIPLY", dx.outputs["Value"], 108)
00357:         dashes = mathnode("SINE", dashes)
00358:         dashes = mathnode("GREATER_THAN", dashes, .55)
00359:         marks = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00360:         marks.operation = "MULTIPLY"
00361:         links.new(rows, marks.inputs[0])
00362:         links.new(dashes, marks.inputs[1])
00363:         marks2 = mathnode("MULTIPLY", marks.outputs[0], .47)
00364:         total = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00365:         total.operation = "MAXIMUM"
00366:         links.new(stripe, total.inputs[0])
00367:         links.new(marks2, total.inputs[1])
00368:         mix = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMixRGB")
00369:         mix.inputs[1].default_value = (*base, 1)
00370:         mix.inputs[2].default_value = (.52, .245, .24, 1)
00371:         links.new(total.outputs[0], mix.inputs[0])
00372:         links.new(mix.outputs[0], bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00373:     return mat

00123: def rounded_outline(x0, x1, y0, y1, radius, z):
00124:     result = []
00125:     for cx, cy, start in [
00126:         (x1-radius, y1-radius, 0),
00127:         (x0+radius, y1-radius, 90),
00128:         (x0+radius, y0+radius, 180),
00129:         (x1-radius, y0+radius, 270)
00130:     ]:
00131:         for j in range(9):
00132:             a = math.radians(start+j*90/8)
00133:             result.append((cx+radius*math.cos(a),
00134:                            cy+radius*math.sin(a), z))
00135:     result.append(result[0])
00136:     return result

00316: def make_fabric(name, base, patterned=False):
00317:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00318:     mat.use_nodes = True
00319:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00320:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00321:     bs = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00322:     bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*base, 1)
00323:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .85
00324:     if "Sheen Weight" in bs.inputs:
00325:         bs.inputs["Sheen Weight"].default_value = .18
00326:     geo = nodes.new("ShaderNodeNewGeometry")
00327:     noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00328:     noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 260
00329:     noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00330:     links.new(geo.outputs["Position"], noise.inputs["Vector"])
00331:     bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00332:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .17
00333:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .0017
00334:     links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00335:     links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00336: 
00337:     if patterned:
00338:         def mathnode(op, a=None, value=None):
00339:             q = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00340:             q.operation = op
00341:             if a is not None:
00342:                 links.new(a, q.inputs[0])
00343:             if value is not None:
00344:                 q.inputs[1].default_value = value
00345:             return q.outputs[0]
00346: 
00347:         dx = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00348:         dx.operation = "DOT_PRODUCT"
00349:         dx.inputs[1].default_value = (.73135, -.682, 0)
00350:         links.new(geo.outputs["Position"], dx.inputs[0])
00351:         dy = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00352:         dy.operation = "DOT_PRODUCT"
00353:         dy.inputs[1].default_value = (.682, .73135, 0)
00354:         links.new(geo.outputs["Position"], dy.inputs[0])
00355: 
00356:         yphase = mathnode("MULTIPLY", dy.outputs["Value"], 138)
00357:         ysin = mathnode("SINE", yphase)
00358:         yabs = mathnode("ABSOLUTE", ysin)
00359:         stripe = mathnode("LESS_THAN", yabs, .055)
00360:         stripe = mathnode("MULTIPLY", stripe, .22)
00361: 
00362:         rows = mathnode("MULTIPLY", dy.outputs["Value"], 69)
00363:         rows = mathnode("SINE", rows)
00364:         rows = mathnode("GREATER_THAN", rows, .94)
00365:         dashes = mathnode("MULTIPLY", dx.outputs["Value"], 108)
00366:         dashes = mathnode("SINE", dashes)
00367:         dashes = mathnode("GREATER_THAN", dashes, .55)
00368:         marks = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00369:         marks.operation = "MULTIPLY"
00370:         links.new(rows, marks.inputs[0])
00371:         links.new(dashes, marks.inputs[1])
00372:         marks2 = mathnode("MULTIPLY", marks.outputs[0], .47)
00373:         total = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00374:         total.operation = "MAXIMUM"
00375:         links.new(stripe, total.inputs[0])
